And further, that on the sixth day of the sacrifice called Daśarātra, at the time of the third pressing, the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa is to combine the two Ṛks beginning "Agne tvam" and the one Ṛk "Taṃ tvā śociṣṭha" into a tṛca (three Ṛks) and recite them as the Ṛks of the stotriya tṛca, is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: अग्ने त्वं नो अंतमोऽग्ने भव सुषमिधा (*agne tvaṃ no aṃtamo 'gne bhava suṣamidhā*) (Ā. 8-2).

##### Particulars of the sūkta

- Sūkta 24
- Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 24
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 16
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4
- Seer (Ṛks 1, 2, 3, 4): Bandhu, Subandhu, Śrutabandhu, Viprabandhu, or Gaupāyanas or Laupāyanas
- Deity: Agni
- Metre: Dvipadā

#### Ṛk 1–2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ त्वं नो॒ अंत॑म उ॒त त्रा॒ता शि॒वो भ॑वा वरू॒थ्यः॑ ।  
> वसु॑र॒ग्निर्वसु॑श्रवा॒ अच्छा॑ नक्षि द्यु॒मत्त॑मं र॒यिं दाः॑ ॥१॥ ॥२॥

### Printed p. 121

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । त्वं । नः । अंतमः । उत । त्राता । शिवः । भव । वरूथ्यः ।

> वसुः । अग्निः । वसुश्रवाः । अच्छ । नक्षि । द्युमत्तमं। रयिं । दाः ॥ १-२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने वरूथ्यो वरणीयः संभजनीयः । यद्वा । वरूथैः परिधिभिर्वृतस्त्वं नोऽस्माकमंतमोऽ०तिकतमो भव । उशापि च त्राता रक्षकः शिवः
> सुखकरश्च भव । वसुर्वासकोऽग्निः सर्वेषामग्रणीर्वसुश्रवा व्याप्तान्नस्त्वमच्छाभिमुख्येन नक्षि । आस्मान्वाप्नुहि । द्युमत्तममतिशयेन दीप्तिमंतं रयिं पश्वादिलक्षणं धनं दाः । अस्मभ्यं देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, who are to be chosen (*varūthya*), that is, to be resorted to; or else, enclosed by encircling fences (*varūthaiḥ*), be nearest to us. Be also our protector and the giver of welfare. Agni is the dweller (*vasu*), the foremost of all; being rich in food (*vasuśravas*), turn towards us and reach us; give us most brilliant wealth, wealth in the form of cattle and the like; bestow it on us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- वरूथ्यः (*varūthyaḥ*)—you who are greatly worshipful
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अंतमः (*aṃtamaḥ*)—exceedingly near
- भव (*bhava*)—be
- उत (*uta*)—and
- त्राता (*trātā*)—our protector
- शिवः (*śivaḥ*)—the bringer of welfare (be)
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—the leader of all, as it were
- वसुः (*vasuḥ*)—the giver of dwellings
- वसुश्रवाः (*vasuśravāḥ*)—you who are the distributor of food
- अच्छ (*accha*)—turned towards us
- नक्षि (*nakṣi*)—be pervading among us
- द्युमत्तमं (*dyumattamaṃ*)—most radiant
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- दाः (*dāḥ*)—give (to us)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O worshipful Agni, be exceedingly near to us, our protector and the bringer of welfare. You, who are the giver of dwellings, the leader of all, as it were, and the distributor of food, be turned towards us and pervade us. Give us most radiant wealth.

##### English translation
Agni, you are to be adored, be ever nigh to us, our protector and benefactor : you, who are the giver of dwellings and dispenser of food, be present with us : bestow upon us most brilliant wealth.

#### Ṛk 3–4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नो॑ बोधि श्रु॒धी हव॑मुरु॒ष्या णो॑ अघाय॒तः स॑मस्मात् ।

### Printed p. 122

> तं त्वा शोचिष्ठ दीदिवः सुम्नाय नूनमीमहे  
> सविभ्यः ॥ ३ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । बोधि । श्रुधि । हवं । उरुष्य । नः । अघ्ययतः । समस्मात् ।

> तं । त्वा । शोचिष्ठ । दीदिवः । सुम्नाय । नूनं । ईमहे । सखिभ्यः ॥३॥४॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने स त्वं नोऽस्मान् बोधि । बुध्यस्व । हवमस्मदीयमाह्वानं श्रुधि ।
> शृणु । अघायतोऽघमिच्छतः समस्मात्सर्वस्माज्जनान्नोऽस्मानुरुष्य । रक्ष । हे
> शोचिष्ठातिशोचिष्मन् दीदिवः स्वशेजोभिर्दिप्ताग्ने तं त्वां सुम्नाय सुखाय ।
> सुम्ममिति सुखनाम । तदर्थं सखिभ्यः समानख्यातिभ्यः पुत्रेभ्यस्तदर्थं च नूनमीमहे । याचामहे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, that you (*sa*) understand us (*bodhi*, "know us"); hear (*śrudhi*) our invocation. Protect (*uruṣya*) us from all men who wish us harm (*aghāyataḥ*, "who desire evil"). O most radiant (*śociṣṭha*), blazing with your own splendours (*dīdivaḥ*), Agni: to you, for the sake of happiness (*sumna*; "*sumna*" is a name of happiness) and for the sake of our friends (*sakhibhyaḥ*), that is, our sons who share one name with us, we now (*nūnam*) make our petition (*īmahe*, "we beg").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सः (*saḥ*)—you who are renowned (O Agni)
- नः (*naḥ*)—us (our devotion)
- बोधि (*bodhi*)—understand
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—our invocation made with praise
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear
- अघायतः (*aghāyataḥ*)—who wish us harm
- समस्तात् (*samastāt*)—from all enemies **NOTE**
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- उरुष्य (*uruṣya*)—protect
- शोचिष्ठ (*śociṣṭha*)—O you of exceeding radiance
- दीदिवः (*dīdivaḥ*)—O Agni, who shine with those radiances
- तं त्वा (*taṃ tvā*)—you, that one
- सुम्नाय (*sumnāya*)—for happiness
- सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)—for the sake of our dear sons
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- ईमहे (*īmahe*)—we praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, renowned as you are, understand our devotion. Hear our invocation made with praise. Protect us from all enemies who wish us harm. O Agni, of exceeding radiance, shining with those radiances, we now praise you for happiness and for the sake of our dear sons.

##### English translation
Understand us, Agni; hear our invocation; defend us from all malevolent (people) : most bright and resplendent Agni, we earnestly solicit you for the happiness (of ourselves) and our friends.

### Printed p. 123

## Sūkta 25 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अच्छा वो अग्निमिति नवर्चमेकादशं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । अच्छा वो
> नव वसूयव आनुष्टुभमिति । आत्रेया वसूयुनामान ऋषयः । अनुष्टुप् ।
> मंडलादिपरिभाषयाग्निर्देवता ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोरुक्तो विनियोगः ॥
> प्रातःसवनेऽच्छावाक वदस्वेत्युक्तेऽच्छा वो अग्निमवस इत्यृचमनुब्रूयात् ।
> तथा च सूत्रितं । अच्छा वो अग्निमवस इत्यृचमन्वाह । आ. ५-७ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Acchā vo agnim" is the eleventh sūkta and has nine Ṛks. Here the Anukramaṇikā says: "Acchā vo nava, Vasūyavas, Ānuṣṭubha"; the seers are the Ātreyas named Vasūyu; the metre is Anuṣṭubh; by the rule at the beginning of the maṇḍala the deity is Agni. The use in the Prātaranuvāka and the Āśvina-śastra has been stated. At the morning pressing, when the Adhvaryu has said "Acchāvāka, speak", the Acchāvāka is to recite after him the Ṛk अच्छा वो अग्निमवस (*acchā vo agnimavasa*). So it is laid down in the sūtra: अच्छा वो अग्निमवस इत्यृचमन्वाह (*acchā vo agnimavasa ityṛcamanvāha*), Ā. 5-7.

This sūkta, "Acchā vo agnim", is the eleventh sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has nine Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: "Acchā vo nava Vasūyava Ānuṣṭubham". The seers of this sūkta are those born in the line of Atri, named Vasūyavas; the deity is Agni; the metre is Anuṣṭubh. It has been said before that this sūkta is used for the Prātaranuvāka and for the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra. And that at the time of the morning pressing, as soon as the Adhvaryu has recited the praiṣa mantra addressed to the priest called Acchāvāka, saying "speak", that priest called Acchāvāka is to recite the Ṛk "Acchā vo agnim avase", is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: अच्छा वो अग्निमवस इत्यृचमन्वाह (*acchā vo agnimavasa ityṛcamanvāha*) (Ā. 5-7).

##### Particulars of the sūkta

- Sūkta 25
- Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 25
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 17, 18
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 9
- Seer: the Vasūyus, of the line of Atri
- Deity: Agni
- Metre: Anuṣṭubh

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अच्छा॑ वो अ॒ग्निमव॑से दे॒वं गा॑सि॒ स नो॒ वसुः॑ ।  
> रास॑त्पु॒त्र ऋ॑षू॒णामृ॒तावा॑ पर्षति द्वि॒षः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 124

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अच्छ । वः । अग्निं । अवसे । देवं । गासि । सः । नः । वसुः ।  
> रासत् । पुत्रः । ऋषूणां । ऋत्यवा । पर्षति । द्विषः ॥१॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वसूयवो वो यूयं देवमग्निमवसे रक्षणायाच्छा गासि । अभिप्रगायत । वसुरग्निहोत्रार्थं यजमानानां गृहे वासयिता सोऽग्निर्नोऽस्मभ्यं
> रासत् । कामान्ददातु । ऋषूणामृषीणां पुत्रः । ऋषिभिर्मंथनेन जनितत्वात्पुत्र
> इत्युपचर्यते । ऋतावा ऋतावान् सत्यवानुदकवान्वा सोऽग्निर्द्विषोऽस्माकं
> शत्रुन्वर्षति । पारयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vasūyus (you who desire wealth), sing forth (*gāsi*, "praise") to the divine Agni for protection. Agni, the dweller (*vasu*), who makes himself dwell in the houses of the sacrificers for the agnihotra, may he (*sa*) grant (*rāsat*) us our desires. He is the son (*putra*) of the seers (*ṛṣūṇām*): because he is produced by the seers by churning, he is called "son" figuratively. Truthful (*ṛtāvā*), that is, possessed of truth, or possessed of water, that Agni may carry us across (*parṣati*) our enemies (*dviṣaḥ*), that is, ferry us over them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वः (*vaḥ*)—you (O seers named Vasūyu)
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—divine in nature
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- अच्छा (*acchā*)—in a commendable manner
- गासि (*gāsi*)—praise and sing
- वसुः (*vasuḥ*)—dwelling in the house of the sacrifice
- सः (*saḥ*)—that Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- रासत् (*rāsat*)—may he grant our desires
- ऋषूणां (*ṛṣūṇāṃ*)—to the seers
- पुत्रः (*putraḥ*)—in the form of a son (born by churning and the like)
- the truthful Agni **NOTE**
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—our enemies
- पर्षति (*parṣati*)—may he destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O seers named Vasūyu, sing the praise of Agni, divine in nature, in a commendable manner, for protection. May that Agni, who dwells in the house of the sacrifice, grant us our desires. May Agni, who, being produced by churning and the like, is as a son to the seers, and who is truthful, destroy our enemies.

##### English translation
Celebrate the divine Agni for his protection : may he who presides over dwellings grant (our desires) : may the son of the Rishis, the observers of truth, save us from those who hate us.

### Printed p. 125

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स हि स॒त्यो यं पूर्वे॑ चिद्दे॒वास॑श्चि॒द्यमा॑धि॒रे ।  
> होता॑रं मं॒द्रजि॑ह्व॒मित्सु॑दी॒तिभि॑र्वि॒भाव॑सुं ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । हि । सत्यः । यं । पूर्वे । चित् । देवासः।चित् । यं । ईधिरे ।  
> होतारं । मंद्रजिह्वं । इत् । सुदीतिभिः । विभावसुं ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वे चित्तूर्वे महर्षयो होतारं देवानामाह्वातारं मंद्रजिह्वं ।
> हविषां प्रदानेन देवानां मादयित्रि जिह्वा यस्य सः । तं । यद्वा मोदनजिह्वं।
> तथाह यास्कः । मंद्रजिह्वं मंदनजिह्वं मोदनजिह्वमिति वा । नि. ६-२३ । इति ।
> सुदीतिभिः शोभनदीप्तिभिर्युक्तं विभावसुं प्रभाधनं यमग्निमीधिरे समैंधत
> देवासश्चिद्देवा अपि यमग्निमीधिरे समैंधत स ह्यग्निः सत्यो भवति । सत्यप्रतिश्रवो
> भवति । इदिति पूरणः ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The ancient (*pūrve cit*) great seers kindled Agni, the invoker (*hotāra*) of the gods, whose tongue gives delight (*mandrajihva*): his tongue delights the gods by the offering of oblations; or else, "of delighting tongue". So Yāska says: मंद्रजिह्वं मंदनजिह्वं मोदनजिह्वमिति वा (*maṃdrajihvaṃ maṃdanajihvaṃ modanajihvamiti vā*), Ni. 6-23. He is joined with fair radiances (*sudītibhiḥ*) and has light as his wealth (*vibhāvasu*). The gods too (*devāsaś cit*) kindled that Agni (*īdhire*, "kindled"); that Agni is indeed true (*satya*): he is true to his promise. The word *it* is a filler.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—the invoker for the whole sacrifice
- मंद्रजिह्वं (*maṃdrajihvaṃ*)—he whose flame, in the form of a tongue, gives delight
- सुदीतिभिः (*sudītibhiḥ*)—he who is joined with commendable brilliance
- विभावसुं (*vibhāvasuṃ*)—he whose wealth is radiance
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Agni
- पूर्वे चित् (*pūrve cit*)—the ancient seers
- ईधिरे (*īdhire*)—kindled
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which same Agni
- देवासश्चित् (*devāsaścit*)—the gods too
- ईधिरे (*īdhire*)—kindled
- स हि (*sa hi*)—that same Agni
- सत्य (*satya*)—is of the nature of truth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni is the invoker for the whole sacrifice; he has a delighting flame in the form of a tongue; he is joined with commendable brilliance; he has radiance as his wealth. The ancient seers and the gods too kindled him; that Agni is truly of the nature of truth.

### Printed p. 126

##### English translation
That Agni is true whom the ancients, whom the gods have kindled, as the bright-tongued invoker of the gods, radiant with holy splendours.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नो॑ धी॒ती वरि॑ष्ठया॒ श्रेष्ठ॑या च सुम॒त्या ।  
> अग्ने॑ रा॒यो दि॑दीहि नः सुवृ॒क्तिभि॑र्वरेण्य ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । धीती । वरिष्ठया । श्रेष्ठया । च । सुमत्या ।

> अग्ने । रायः । दिदीहि । नः । सुवृक्तिभिः । वरेण्य ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुवृक्तिभिः स्तुतिभिः स्तूयमान वरेण्य वरणीय हे अग्ने स त्वं वरिष्ठया
> वरतमेन श्रेष्ठ यातिशयेन प्रशस्येन नो धीती धीत्यास्मदीयेन परिचरणात्मकेन
> कर्मणा सुमत्या च । सुमतिरिति शस्त्रमुच्यते । शस्त्रेण च प्रीतः सन् रायो धनानि
> नोऽस्मभ्यं दीदिहि । देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, praised with fair praises (*suvṛktibhiḥ*, "with hymns") and worthy of being chosen (*vareṇya*): you, with our best (*variṣṭhayā*, "most choice") and most excellent, praiseworthy (*śreṣṭhayā*) service (*dhītī*, our act of attendance), and with our *sumati*, which here means a śastra, being pleased with the śastra, give (*dīdihi*) us riches (*rāyaḥ*, "wealth").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुवृक्तिभिः (*suvṛktibhiḥ*)—he who is praised with excellent praises
- वरेण्य (*vareṇya*)—he who is greatly honoured
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सः (*saḥ*)—you who are renowned
- वरिष्ठया (*variṣṭhayā*)—with what is the very best
- श्रेष्ठया (*śreṣṭhayā*)—and praiseworthy
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- धीती (*dhītī*)—with acts worthy of worship
- सुमत्या च (*sumatyā ca*)—and with praises in the form of śastras (being gratified)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—riches
- दीदिहि (*dīdihi*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, praised with excellent praises and greatly honoured, renowned as you are, be gratified with our very best and praiseworthy acts worthy of worship and with our praises in the form of śastras, and give us riches.

### Printed p. 127

##### English translation
Agni, to be propitiated by praises, (gratified) by our choice and most excellent adoration and hymns, bestow upon us riches.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्दे॒वेषु॑ राजत्य॒ग्निर्मर्ते॑ष्वावि॒शन् ।  
> अ॒ग्निर्नो॑ हव्य॒वाह॑नो॒ऽग्निं धी॒भिः स॑पर्यत ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । देवेषु । राजति । अग्निः । मर्तॆषु । आविशन् ।  
> अग्निः । नः । हव्यवाहनः । अग्निं । धीभिः । सपर्यत ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> योऽग्निर्देवेषु देवानां मध्ये राजति देवतारूपेण प्रकाशते । योऽग्निर्मर्तेषु मनुष्यॆष्टाविशन् आहवनीयादिरूपेण प्रविष्टो भवति । योऽग्निर्नोऽस्माकं हव्यवाहनो भवति अस्मदीयेषु यज्ञेषु देवतार्थं हव्यानां
> वोढा भवति हे यजमानास्तमग्निं धीभिः स्तुतिभिः सपर्यत । परिचरत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni who shines (*rājati*) among the gods, that is, appears in the form of a deity in their midst; the Agni who has entered (*āviśan*) among mortals (*marteṣu*), that is, men, in the form of the Āhavanīya and the other fires; the Agni who is the bearer of our oblations (*havyavāhana*), that is, the carrier of the oblations to the deities in our sacrifices: O sacrificers, serve (*saparyata*, "attend upon") that Agni with praises (*dhībhiḥ*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—among the gods
- राजति (*rājati*)—shines in the form of a deity
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- मर्तेषु (*marteṣu*)—among men
- आविशन् (*āviśan*)—has entered in the form of the Āhavanīya and the others
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- हव्यवाहनः (*havyavāhanaḥ*)—is the bearer of the oblation (to the gods)
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—such a worshipful Agni (O sacrificers)
- धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)—with praises
- सपर्यत (*saparyata*)—worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The worshipful Agni shines among the gods in the form of a deity. Agni has entered among men in the form of the Āhavanīya and the other fires. That same Agni is for us the bearer of the oblation to the gods. O sacrificers, worship such a worshipful Agni with praises.

### Printed p. 128

##### English translation
Agni shines amongst the gods: Agni is present amongst mortals : Agni is the bearer of our oblations : glorify Agni with praises.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निस्तु॒ विश्र॑वस्तमं तु॒विब्र॑ह्माणमुत्त॒मं ।  
> अ॒तूर्तं॑ श्राव॒यत्प॑तिं पु॒त्रं द॑दाति दा॒शुषे॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । तुविश्रवःतमं । तुविब्रह्माणं । उत्तमं ।  
> अतूर्तं । श्रवयत् पतिं । पुत्रं । ददाति । दाशुषे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुत्रकामेष्ट्यामग्निस्तु विश्रवस्तममिति द्वे स्विष्टकृतो याज्यानुवाक्ये ।
> सूत्रितं च । अग्निस्तुविश्रवस्तममिति द्वे संयाज्ये । आ. २-१० । इति ॥
> अग्निस्तु विश्रवस्तममतिशयेन बह्वन्नं तुविब्रह्माणं बहुस्तोत्रमुत्तममुत्कृष्टमतूर्तं शत्रुभिरहिंसितं श्रावयत्पतिं । श्रावयति विश्रुतान् करोति
> पतीन् पालयित्थन्पित्यनिति स्वकर्मणा पित्यणां प्रख्यापक इति श्रावयत्पतिः । तथाविधं पुत्रं दाशुषे हवींषि दत्तवते यजमानाय ददाति । ददातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Putrakāmeṣṭi (the offering for the wish for a son), the two Ṛks beginning "Agnis tu viśravastamam" are the yājyā and the anuvākyā of the Sviṣṭakṛt. So it is laid down in the sūtra: अग्निस्तुविश्रवस्तममिति द्वे संयाज्ये (*agnistuviśravastamamiti dve saṃyājye*), Ā. 2-10. Agni, who is of exceeding great fame in food (*viśravastama*, "exceedingly abounding in food"), of many praises (*tuvibrahmāṇa*, "of much hymning"), most excellent (*uttama*), unharmed by enemies (*atūrta*), and *śrāvayatpati*: he makes the lords (*pati*) famous (*śrāvayati*), that is, by his own deeds he makes the fathers known, hence *śrāvayatpati*; **NOTE** may Agni give such a son to the sacrificer (*dāśuṣe*) who has given oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the sacrificer who offers the oblation
- तुविक्रवस्तमं (*tuvikravastamaṃ*)—endowed with very abundant wealth of food **NOTE**
- तुविब्रह्माणं (*tuvibrahmāṇaṃ*)—who composes praise in great measure
- उत्तमं (*uttamaṃ*)—possessed of excellent qualities
- अतूर्तं (*atūrtaṃ*)—not harmed by enemies
- श्रवयत्पतिं (*śravayatpatiṃ*)—who by his excellent deeds brings fame to all his fathers, grandfathers and the rest
- पुत्रं (*putraṃ*)—a son
- ददाति (*dadāti*)—may he give

### Printed p. 129

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Agni favour the sacrificer who offers the oblation with a son who is endowed with very abundant wealth of food, who in great measure composes praises of the deities, who is possessed of excellent qualities, who is not harmed by enemies, and who by his excellent deeds brings fame to all his fathers, grandfathers and the rest.

##### English translation
May Agni bestow upon the donor (of the oblation) a son, abounding in food, abounding in devotion, excellent, unharmed, conferring honour upon his progenitors.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the sacrifice called Putrakāmeṣṭi, this Ṛk "Agnis tu viśravastamam" and the Ṛk after it are to be recited as the yājyā and anuvākyā mantras in the Sviṣṭakṛt homa, as is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: अग्निस्तु विश्रवस्तममिति द्वे संयाज्ये (*agnistu viśravastamamiti dve saṃyājye*) (Ā. 2-10).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्द॑दाति॒ सत्प॑तिं सा॒साह॒ यो यु॒धा नृभिः॑ ।  
> अ॒ग्निरत्यं॑ रघु॒ष्यदं॒ जीता॑र॒मप॑राजितं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । ददाति । सत्पतिं । ससाह । यः । युधा । नृभिः ।  
> अग्निः । अत्यं । रघुस्यदं । जेतारं । अपराजितं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यः पुत्रो युधा युद्धेन नृभिः परिजनैः ससाह शत्रुनभिभवति सत्पतिं
> सतां पालयितारं तथाविधं पुत्रमग्निर्ददाति । अस्मभ्यं ददातु । किंचाग्नी रघुष्यदं। रघुर्लघुः स्यदो जवो यस्य तं । जेतारं शत्रुणामपराजितं शत्रुभिरत्यं।
> अतति सततं गच्छतीत्यत्तोऽश्वः । तं ददातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The son who in battle (*yudhā*), with his men (*nṛbhiḥ*, his retinue), overcomes (*sasāha*) his enemies, the protector of the good (*satpati*): Agni gives such a son; may he give him to us. Further, Agni (may give) a horse that is *raghuṣyada*: *raghu* is light, *syada* is speed, he whose speed is light; a conqueror (*jetāra*) of enemies, unconquered (*aparājita*) by enemies; *atya* is the horse (*atta*), because it goes (*atati*) constantly. May he give that.

### Printed p. 130

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which son
- युधा (*yudhā*)—in battles
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—with his followers
- ससाह (*sasāha*)—defeats the enemies, such a one
- सत्पतिं (*satpatiṃ*)—and the protector of the good, such a son
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- ददाति (*dadāti*)—may he favour us with and give
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—that same Agni
- रघुष्यदं (*raghuṣyadaṃ*)—swift-going
- जेतारं (*jetāraṃ*)—conquering (enemies)
- अपराजितं (*aparājitaṃ*)—unconquered (by enemies)
- अत्यं (*atyaṃ*)—a horse (may he give)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Agni favour us with a son who defeats enemies in battles together with his followers and who is the protector of the good. May that same Agni give a horse that is swift-going, conquering enemies, and unconquered by enemies.

##### English translation
May Agni bestow a son, the protector of the good, who, with his followers, may be victorious in battle : may he bestow a swift-footed steed, conquering, unconquered.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यद्वाहि॑ष्ठं॒ तद॒ग्नये॑ बृ॒हद॑र्च विभावसो ।  
> महि॑षीव॒ त्वद्र॒यिस्त्वद्वाजा॒ उदी॑रते ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । वाहिष्ठं । तत् । अग्नये । बृहत् । अर्च । विभावसो इति विभावसो ।  
> महिषीs इव । त्वत् । रयिः । त्वत् । वाजाः । उत् । ईरते ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अश्वमेधे पौष्णा मिष्ट यद्वाहिष्ठमिति स्विष्टकृतो याज्या । सूत्रितं च ।
> यद्वाहिष्ठं तदग्नय इति संयाज्ये । आ. १०-६ इति ॥
> वाहिष्ठं वोडृतमं यत्तोत्रं तदग्नये क्रियते । अतो हे विधावसो
> प्रभाधनाग्ने बृहद्बृहन्नं धनं वार्च । अस्मभ्यं प्रयच्छ । कथमस्यान्नधनप्रदातृत्व-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

At the Aśvamedha, in the offering to Pūṣan (**NOTE**), the Ṛk "Yad vāhiṣṭham" is the yājyā of the Sviṣṭakṛt. So it is laid down in the sūtra: यद्वाहिष्ठं तदग्नय इति संयाज्ये (*yadvāhiṣṭhaṃ tadagnaya iti saṃyājye*), Ā. 10-6. That hymn (*stotra*) which is best at carrying (*vāhiṣṭha*, "most conveying") is made for Agni. Therefore, O Agni, whose wealth is light (*vibhāvaso*; **NOTE**), give us abundant (*bṛhat*) food or wealth (*arca*, "give"). If it is asked how he can be the giver of food and wealth, he says: because from you

### Printed p. 131

> मित्यपेक्षायामाह । यतस्त्वत्त्वत्तः सकाशान्महिषी महती रयिर्धनमुदीरते
> उद्गच्छति । वाजा अन्नानि च त्वत्त्वत्त उदीरते उद्गच्छंति । इवेति पूरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

... from you great (*mahiṣī*) wealth (*rayi*) rises up (*udīrate*), and foods (*vājāḥ*) also rise up from you. The word *iva* is a filler.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—which hymn
- वाहिष्ठं (*vāhiṣṭhaṃ*)—carries our devotion in the highest degree
- तत् (*tat*)—that
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—is offered to Agni
- विभावसो (*vibhāvaso*)—O Agni, whose wealth is radiance
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundant wealth
- अर्च (*arca*)—give to us (for)
- त्वत् (*tvat*)—from you alone
- महिषी (*mahiṣī*)—abundant
- रयिः (*rayiḥ*)—wealth
- उदीरते (*udīrate*)—comes forth
- वाजाः (*vājāḥ*)—foods also
- त्वत् (*tvat*)—arise from you and spread

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That hymn which carries our devotion in the highest degree is offered to Agni. O Agni, whose wealth is radiance, give us abundant wealth, for abundant wealth and foods arise from you alone and spread forth.

##### English translation
That (praise) which best conveys (our veneration, is due) to Agni : affluent in splendour, grant us, (Agn'), great (wealth), for from you vast riches and ample food proceed.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Pauṣṭa offering (**NOTE**) of the Aśvamedha sacrifice, this Ṛk "Yad vāhiṣṭham" is to be recited as the yājyā mantra of the smitakṛt homa **NOTE**, as is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: यद्वाहिष्ठं तदग्नय इति संयाज्ये (*yadvāhiṣṭhaṃ tadagnaya iti saṃyājye*) (Ā. 10-6).

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तव॑ द्यु॒मंतो॑ अ॒र्चयो॒ ग्रावे॑वोच्यते बृ॒हत् ।  
> उ॒तो ते॑ तन्य॒तुर्य॑था स्ना॒नो अ॑र्त॒ त्मना॑ दि॒वः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तव । द्युमंतः । अर्चयः । ग्रावाइव । उच्यते । बृहत् ।  
> उतो इति । ते । तन्यतुः । यथा । स्वानः । अर्त । त्मना । दिवः ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 132

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने तवार्चयो रश्मयो द्युमंतो दीप्तिमंतो भवंति । बृहन्महान् त्वं ग्रावेवाभिषवग्रावेवोच्यते । स्तूयसे । उतो अपि च त्मनात्मना दिवो
> द्योतमानस्य ते तव स्नानः शब्दस्तन्यतुर्यथा मेघगर्जितमिनार्त । उद्गच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, your rays (*arcayaḥ*) are radiant (*dyumantaḥ*), full of light. You, who are great (*bṛhat*), are praised (*ucyate*) like the pressing stone (*grāveva*, the stone for pressing). And of you, who shine from the sky (*divaḥ*), by your own power (*tmanā*) your sound (*snānaḥ*) rises (*arta*, "goes up") like thunder (*tanyatuḥ*), the rumbling of a cloud.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- अर्चयः (*arcayaḥ*)—your rays
- द्युमंतः (*dyumaṃtaḥ*)—are full of radiance
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—you, who are of great nature
- ग्रावेव (*grāveva*)—like the stone that presses Soma
- उच्यते (*ucyate*)—are praised
- उतो (*uto*)—and
- त्मना (*tmanā*)—by your own greatness
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—shining
- ते (*te*)—your
- स्नानः (*snānaḥ*)—sound
- तन्यतुः यथा (*tanyatuḥ yathā*)—like the rumbling of a cloud
- अर्त (*arta*)—arises and spreads

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, your rays are full of radiance. You, who are of great nature, are praised like the stone that presses Soma. And your sound, shining by your own greatness, arises and spreads like the rumbling of a cloud.

##### English translation
Brilliant, Agni, are your rays : mighty are you termed, like the stone (that bruises the Soma), and your voice spontaneously spreads through the sky like thunder.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा अ॒ग्निं व॑सू॒यवः॑ सहसा॒नं ॑नवंदिम ।  
> स नो॒ विश्वा॒ अति॒ द्विषः॒ पर्ष॑न्ना॒वेव॑ सु॒क्रतुः॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । अग्निं । वसुयवः । सहसानं । ववंदिम ।  
> सः । नः । विश्वाः । अति । द्विषः । पर्षत् । नावाइव । सुक्रतुः ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 133

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसूयवो वसुकामा वयं सहसानमस्माकं बलमिवाचरंतमग्निमेवमुक्तप्रकारेण ववंदिम । स्तुमः । सुक्रतुः शोभनकर्मा सोऽग्निर्नोऽस्मान्
> विश्वाः सर्वान् द्विषः शत्रूनति पर्षत् । अतिपारयतु । कथमिव । नावा साधनेन
> सिंधुमिव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We, the Vasūyus (*vasūyavaḥ*, those who desire wealth), have thus praised (*vavandima*, "we praise") in the manner stated Agni, who is *sahasāna*, that is, who acts like our own strength. May that Agni, the doer of fair works (*sukratu*), carry us over (*parṣat*, "take us across") all (*viśvāḥ*) our enemies (*dviṣaḥ*). How? As with a boat (*nāvā*), a means, one crosses a river.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसूयवः (*vasūyavaḥ*)—we, who desire wealth (the seers named Vasūyu)
- सहसानं (*sahasānaṃ*)—who accomplishes works endowed with strength
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- ववंदिम (*vavaṃdima*)—we praise
- नाना इव (*nānā iva*)—as one easily crosses a river with a boat **NOTE**
- सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*)—the doer of excellent works
- सः (*saḥ*)—that Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)—all
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—from enemies
- अति पर्षत् (*ati parṣat*)—may he carry across

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We, who are named Vasūyu and desire wealth, praise Agni, who can accomplish works endowed with strength. As one easily crosses a river with a boat, may Agni, the doer of excellent works, carry us across all enemies and protect us.

##### English translation
Thus we, Vasuyus, glorify the vigorous Agni : may he, the performer of great deeds, enable us to pass over our enemies as (we cross a river) with a boat.

## Sūkta 26 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्ने पावकेति नवर्चं द्वादशं सूक्तं । वसूयव ऋषयः । गायत्री छंदः ।
> अग्निर्देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । अग्ने गायत्रमिति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेये क्रत्वा
> गायत्रे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । अग्ने पावक दूतं व इति
> सूक्ते । आ. ४-१३ । इति ॥ आधाने द्वितीयायामिष्टौ प्रथमस्य हविषोऽग्ने पावकेत्येषा याज्या । सूत्रितं च । स नः पावक दीदिवोऽग्ने पावक रोचिषा ।
> आ. २.१ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Agne pāvaka" is the twelfth sūkta and has nine Ṛks. The seers are the Vasūyus, the metre is Gāyatrī, the deity is Agni. So the Anukramaṇikā has it: "Agne, gāyatram". This sūkta is used in the Prātaranuvāka, for the Gāyatrī Ṛks of the Āgneya kratu, and in the Āśvina-śastra. So it is laid down in the sūtra: अग्ने पावक दूतं व इति सूक्ते (*agne pāvaka dūtaṃ va iti sūkte*), Ā. 4-13. Further, at the Ādhāna, in the second iṣṭi, the Ṛk "Agne pāvaka" is the yājyā for the first oblation. So it is laid down: स नः पावक दीदिवोऽग्ने पावक रोचिषा (*sa naḥ pāvaka dīdivo 'gne pāvaka rociṣā*), Ā. 2.1.

### Printed p. 134

This sūkta, "Agne pāvaka", is the twelfth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has nine Ṛks. The seers of this sūkta are those named Vasūyu; the deity is Agni; the metre is Gāyatrī. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: "Agne, gāyatram". That this sūkta is used for reciting the Gāyatrī Ṛks connected with the Āgneya kratu in the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka mantras, and for reciting the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra, is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: अग्ने पावक दूतं व इति सूक्ते (*agne pāvaka dūtaṃ va iti sūkte*) (A. 4-13) **NOTE**. And that at the time of the Ādhāna, when the first oblation in the second iṣṭi is offered, the Ṛk "Agne pāvaka" is to be used as the yājyā mantra, is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: स नः पावक दीदिवोऽग्ने पावक रोचिषा (*sa naḥ pāvaka dīdivo 'gne pāvaka rociṣā*) (Ā. 2-1).

##### Particulars of the sūkta

- Sūkta 26
- Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 26
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 19, 20
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 9
- Seer: the Vasūyus, of the line of Atri
- Deity: Agni
- Metre: Gāyatrī

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॑ पावक रो॒चिषा॑ मं॒द्रया॑ देव जि॒ह्वया॑ ।  
> आ दे॒वान्व॑क्षि॒ यक्षि॑ च ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । पावक । रोचिषा । मंद्रया । देव । जिह्वया ।  
> आ । देवान् । वक्षि । यक्षि । च ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पावक शोधक रोचिषा स्वदीप्ता मंद्रया देवानां मादयित्र्या
> जिह्वया च हे देव द्योतमानाग्ने देवाना वक्षि । यज्ञार्थमावह । यक्षि च ।
> शान्यज ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O purifier (*pāvaka*), O shining (*deva*) Agni, with your own radiance (*rociṣā*) and with your tongue (*jihvayā*) that delights the gods (*mandrayā*), bring (*vakṣi*) the gods for the sacrifice, and sacrifice (*yakṣi*) also. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 135

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पावक (*pāvaka*)—O purifier
- देव (*deva*)—of divine nature
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- रोचिषा (*rociṣā*)—with flames that are radiant
- मंद्रया (*maṃdrayā*)—delighting
- जिह्वया (*jihvayā*)—and in the form of tongues
- देवान् (*devān*)—the gods
- आ वक्षि (*ā vakṣi*)—bring to the sacrifice
- यक्षि च (*yakṣi ca*)—and sacrifice to them

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, purifier and of divine nature, with flames that are radiant, delighting and in the form of tongues, bring the gods to the sacrifice. Sacrifice to them.

##### English translation
Divine purifier, Agni, with your radiant and pleasing tongue, bring hither and worship the gods.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं त्वा॑ धृतस्नवीमहे॒ चित्र॑भानो स्व॒र्दृशं॑ ।  
> दे॒वा आ वी॒तये॑ वह ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । त्वा । घृतस्नो इति घृतस्नो । ईमहे । चित्रभानो इति चित्रभानो ।  
> स्वःसृशं ।  
> देवान् । आ । वीतये । वह ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हॆ धृतस्नो धृतस्य प्रेरक । यद्वा । धृतेन जनित । हे चित्रभानो । चित्रा
> नानाविधा भानवो रश्मयो यस्यासौ चित्रभानुः । तस्य संबोधनं । स्वर्दृशं
> सर्वद्रष्टारं तं त्वा त्वामीमहे । याचामहे । अतो वीतये हविषां भक्षणाय
> देवाना वह ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O *dhṛtasnu*, impeller of ghee (*dhṛta*); or else, born of ghee. O *citrabhānu*: *citrā* means of many kinds, *bhānu* means rays; he who has these is Citrabhānu; this is the address to him. You who see everything (*svardṛśam*), we beg (*īmahe*, "we ask") you; therefore bring the gods for the eating of the oblations (*vītaye*).

### Printed p. 136

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- धृतस्नो (*dhṛtasno*)—he who is kindled by ghee
- चित्रभानो (*citrabhāno*)—O Agni, who has rays of many kinds
- स्वर्दृशं (*svardṛśaṃ*)—who can see everything
- तं (*taṃ*)—such a renowned one
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- ईवंहे (*īvaṃhe*)—we pray **NOTE**
- वीतये (*vītaye*)—to eat the oblation
- देवान् (*devān*)—the gods
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, kindled by ghee and having rays of many kinds, we pray to you, who can see everything and are renowned. Bring the gods to eat the oblation.

##### English translation
Feeder upon butter, bright and variegated radiance we solicit you, the beholder of heaven, to bring the Gods hither to (partake of) the (sacrificial) food

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वी॒तिहो॑त्रं त्वा कवे द्यु॒मंतं॒ समि॑धीमहि ।  
> अग्ने॑ बृ॒हंत॑मध्व॒रे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वीतिहोत्रं । त्वा । कवे । द्युमंतं । सं । इधीमहि ।  
> अग्ने । बृहंतं । अध्वरे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे कवे क्रांतदर्शिन्नग्ने वीतिहोत्रं कांतयज्ञं द्युमंतं दीप्तिमंतं
> बृहंतं महांतं त्वा त्वामध्वरे यज्ञे समिधीमहि । समिद्धिः संदीपयामः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O seer (*kave*), O Agni of far-reaching sight, you whose sacrifice is desired (*vītihotra*), radiant (*dyumantam*) and great (*bṛhantam*): we kindle you (*samidhīmahi*), we make you blaze up, with fuel at the sacrifice (*adhvare*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कवे (*kave*)—O one of far-reaching wisdom
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- वीतिहोत्रं (*vītihotraṃ*)—whose food is the oblation
- द्युमंतं (*dyumaṃtaṃ*)—endowed with radiance
- बृहंतं (*bṛhaṃtaṃ*)—of great nature
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- आध्वरे (*ādhvare*)—at the sacrifice **NOTE**
- समीधिमहि (*samīdhimahi*)—we make blaze up **NOTE**

### Printed p. 137

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, of far-reaching wisdom, whose food is the oblation, endowed with radiance and of great nature, we make you blaze up at the sacrifice.

##### English translation
We kindle you at the sacrifice, wise Agni, whose food is the oblation , who are brilliant and vast.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ विश्वे॑भि॒रा ग॑हि दे॒वेभि॑र्ह॒व्यदा॑तये ।  
> ।  
> होता॑रं त्वा वृणीमहे ॥ ४ ॥

**NOTE**

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । विश्वेभिः । आ । गहि । देवेभिः । हव्यदातये ।  
> होतारं । त्वा । वृणीमहे ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने विश्वेभिः सर्वैर्देवेभिर्देवैः सह त्वं हव्यदातये हविषां दात्रे
> यजमानाय तदर्थमा गहि । यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छ । यतो होतारं देवानामाह्वातारं त्वा त्वां वृणीमहे । प्रार्थयामहे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, come (*ā gahi*) together with all (*viśvebhiḥ*) the gods, to the sacrificer who gives the oblations (*havyadātaye*), for his sake; come to the sacrifice. For we choose (*vṛṇīmahe*, "we pray to") you, the invoker (*hotāra*) of the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- विश्वेभिः (*viśvebhiḥ*)—all
- देवेभिः (*devebhiḥ*)—together with the gods
- हव्यदातये (*havyadātaye*)—to the sacrificer who offers the oblation
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be gracious to come
- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—who are the invoker of the gods
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- वृणीमहे (*vṛṇīmahe*)—we pray to

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, we pray to you, the invoker of the gods, saying: be gracious to come, together with all the gods, to the sacrificer who offers the oblation.

##### English translation
Come, Agni, with all the gods, to the donor (of the oblation) : we have recourse to you as their invoker.

### Printed p. 138

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यज॑मानाय सुन्व॒त आग्ने॑ सु॒वीर्यं॑ वह ।  
> दे॒वैरा स॑त्सि ब॒र्हिषि॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यजमानाय । सुन्वते । आ । अग्ने । सुवीर्यं । वह ।  
> देवैः । आ । सत्सि । बर्हिषि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सुन्वतेऽभिषवं कुर्वते यजमानाय सुवीर्यं शोभनं बलमा
> वह । प्रापय । किंच देवैः सह बर्हिषि यज्ञ आ सत्सि । आसीदेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, bring (*ā vaha*, "cause to reach") fair strength (*suvīryam*) to the sacrificer (*yajamānāya*) who presses the Soma (*sunvate*). Further, sit down (*ā satsi*, "sit") together with the gods on the sacrificial grass (*barhiṣi*), that is, at the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सुन्वते (*sunvate*)—who presses the Soma juice
- यजमानाय (*yajamānāya*)—to the sacrificer
- सुवीरं (*suvīraṃ*)—excellent strength **NOTE**
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—cause to be obtained
- देवैः (*devaiḥ*)—together with the gods
- बर्हिषि (*barhiṣi*)—on the seats of darbha grass
- आ सत्सि (*ā satsi*)—sit down

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, cause the sacrificer who presses the Soma juice to obtain excellent strength. Come with the gods and sit down on the seats of darbha grass.

##### English translation
To the institutor of the rite, pouring out the libation, bring excellent vigour ; sit down with the gods upon the sacred grass.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒मि॒धा॒नः स॑हस्रजि॒दग्ने॒ धर्मा॑णि पुष्यसि ।  
> दे॒वानां॑ दू॒त उ॒क्तः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 139

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइधानः । सहस्रजित् । अग्ने । धर्माणि । पुष्यसि ।  
> देवानां । दूतः । उक्ष्यः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सहस्रजित्सहस्रस्य जेतरग्ने समिधानो हविर्भिः समिध्यॆमान उक्त्यः
> प्रशस्यस्त्वं देवानां दूतः सन् धर्माणि नः कर्माणि यज्ञादिक्रियाः पुष्यसि ॥
> पोषयसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses Agni as "victor of a thousand" (the winner of a thousand), kindled with oblations and praiseworthy. As the messenger of the gods, he says, Agni nourishes our *dharmāṇi*, that is, our rites such as the sacrifice; *puṣyasi* means "you cause to thrive".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहस्रजित् (*sahasrajit*)—one who has won victory in thousands of situations
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- समिधानः (*samidhānaḥ*)—kindled with oblations
- उक्थ्यः (*ukthyaḥ*)—praised
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of the gods
- दूतः (*dūtaḥ*)—as messenger
- धर्माणि (*dharmāṇi*)—our sacrificial and other rites
- पुष्यसि (*puṣyasi*)—you cause to thrive

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who have won victory in thousands of situations, kindled with oblations and worthy of praise, you, as messenger of the gods, cause our sacrificial and other good rites to thrive.

##### English translation
Victor over thousands, you favour, when kindled, our holy rites, the honoured messenger of the gods.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न्य१॒॑ग्निं जा॒तवे॑दसं होत्र॒वाहं॒ यवि॑ष्ठ्यं ।  
> दधाता देवमृत्तिजं ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नि । अग्निं । जातवेदसं । होत्रवाहं । यविष्ठ्यं ।  
> दधात । देवं । ऋत्विजं ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 140

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे यजमाना जातवेदसं । जातानि भूतानि वेत्तीति जातवेदाः । तं
> होत्रवाहं होत्रस्य यज्ञस्य वोढारं यविष्ठ्यं युवतमं देवं द्योतमानमृत्विजन्मृतौ यष्टारमग्निं नि दधात । निधत्त ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses the sacrificers: "Establish Agni." He is *Jātavedas*, because he knows all beings that are born; the bearer of the *hotra*, that is, the carrier of the sacrifice; the youngest; the god, that is, the shining one; the *ṛtvij*, the one who sacrifices in the proper season. *Nidadhāta* means "establish".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—him who knows all beings that have come into being
- होत्रवाहं (*hotravāhaṃ*)—who carries the oblations (to the gods)
- यविष्ठ्यं (*yaviṣṭhyaṃ*)—the very youthful
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—the shining
- ऋत्विजं (*ṛtvijaṃ*)—who is the chief priest of the sacrifice
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- निदधात (*nidadhāta*)—establish and worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sacrificers, establish and worship Agni, who knows all beings that have come into being, who carries the oblations to the gods, who is very youthful and shining, and who is the chief priest of the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Reverence Angi, by whom all that is, is known, the bearer of oblations the youngest of the gods, the divine, the ministrant priest.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र य॒ज्ञ ए॑त्वानु॒षग॒द्या दे॒वव्य॑चस्तमः ।  
> स्त्री॒णी॒त ब॒र्हिरा॒सदे॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । यज्ञः । एतु । आनुषक् । अद्य । देवव्यचःतमः ।  
> स्त्रीणीत । बर्हिः । आसदे ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अद्यास्मिन्नहनि देवव्यचस्तमो देवैः प्रकाशमानैः स्तोतृभिर्व्याप्ततमो
> यज्ञो यज्ञसाधनं हविरानुषगनुषक्तं यथा भवति तथा प्रैतु । देवान्
> गच्छतु । हे ऋत्विज आसदेऽग्नेरासदनार्थं बर्हिः स्मृणीत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Today, on this day, let the sacrifice, which is the means of sacrifice, namely the oblation, most pervaded by the shining praisers of the gods, go forth uninterrupted to the gods. O priests, spread the sacred grass for Agni's seat.

### Printed p. 141

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्य (*adya*)—in this sacrifice
- देवव्यचस्तमः (*devavyacastamaḥ*)—offered with the utmost devotion by the shining praisers **NOTE**
- यज्ञः (*yajñaḥ*)—the oblation
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—duly, according to rule
- प्र एतु (*pra etu*)—let it reach the gods (O priests)
- आसदे (*āsade*)—for Agni's seat
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—the seat of darbha grass
- स्तणीत (*staṇīta*)—spread

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In this sacrifice, let the oblation offered with the utmost devotion by the shining praisers duly reach the gods. O priests, spread the seat of darbha grass for Agni.

##### English translation
May the sacrifice most solemnly offered by the devout duly proceed to-day (to the gods) : spread the sacred grass for their seat.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विदं म॒रुतो॑ अ॒श्विना॑ मि॒त्रः सी॑दंतु॒ वरु॑णः ।  
> दे॒वासः॒ सर्व॑या वि॒शा ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इदं । मरुतः । अश्विना । मित्रः । सीदंतु । वरुणः ।  
> देवासः । सर्वया । विशा ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मरुतो मरुद्गणा अश्विनाश्विन् देवानां भिषजौ मित्रः सूर्यो वरुणश्च
> देवास एते सर्वे देवाः सर्वया विशा समस्तेन स्वीयेन परिजनेन सार्धमिदं
> बर्हिरा सीदंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts are the troops of Maruts; the Aśvins are the physicians of the gods; Mitra is the sun; and Varuṇa. All these gods, together with their entire retinue, are to sit down on this sacred grass.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Marut gods
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin gods
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—all these gods
- सर्वया विशा (*sarvayā viśā*)—with all the attendant gods belonging to them
- इदं (*idaṃ*)—upon these seats of darbha grass
- आ सीदंतु (*ā sīdaṃtu*)—let them sit

### Printed p. 142

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the Maruts, who are of divine nature, the Aśvin gods, Mitra and Varuṇa, together with all the attendant gods belonging to them, come and sit down upon these seats of darbha grass.

##### English translation
May the Maruts, the Ashwins, Mitra, Varuna, the gods, with all their attendants, sit down upon this grass.

## Sūkta 27 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-seventh sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनस्वंतेति षडृचं त्रयोदशं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । अनस्वंता षट्
> त्रैवृष्णपौरुकुत्सौ द्वा त्रैरुणत्रसदस्य राजान् भारतश्चाश्वमेधोऽंत्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभो नात्मात्मने दद्यादिति सर्वास्वत्रिं केचिदंत्यैंद्राग्नीति । त्रिवृष्टस्य
> पुत्रस्त्र्यरुणः पुरुकुत्सस्य पुत्रस्त्रसदस्युर्भरतस्य पुत्रोऽश्वमेध एते त्रयोऽपि
> राजानः संभूयास्य सूक्तस्य ऋषयः । यद्वा । अत्रिरेव ऋषिः । अद्यास्तिस्रस्त्रिष्टुभश्चतुर्थाद्यास्ति स्रोऽनुष्टुभः । षष्ठींद्राग्नि देवत्या शिष्टाः पंचाग्नेय्यः ॥ विनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning *anasvantā* has six Ṛks. Sāyaṇa cites the Anukramaṇikā for its seers, metres and deities (quoted in full in the Anuvāda below). He explains that Tryaruṇa, son of Trivṛṣṭa, Trasadasyu, son of Purukutsa, and Aśvamedha, son of Bharata, these three kings together are the seers of this sūkta; or else Atri alone is the seer. **NOTE** The first three Ṛks are in the Triṣṭubh metre, the three beginning with the fourth are in Anuṣṭubh. The sixth has Indra-Agni as deity; the remaining five are addressed to Agni. The use in ritual is by indication (*laiṅgika*).

The sūkta beginning *anasvantā* is the thirteenth sūkta in the second Anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: अनस्वंता षट् त्रैवृष्णपौरुकुत्सौ द्वा त्रैरुणत्रसदस्य राजान् भारतश्चाश्वमेधोऽंत्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभो नात्मात्मने दद्यादिति सर्वास्वत्रिं केचिदंत्यैंद्राग्नीति । त्रिवृष्टस्य पुत्रस्त्र्यरुणः पुरुकुत्सस्य पुत्रस्त्रसदस्युर्भरतस्य पुत्रोऽश्वमेध एते त्रयोऽपि राजानः संभूयास्य सूक्तस्य ऋषयः । यद्वा । अत्रिरेव ऋषिः । अद्यास्तिस्रस्त्रिष्टुभश्चतुर्थाद्यास्ति स्रोऽनुष्टुभः । षष्ठींद्राग्नि देवत्या शिष्टाः पंचाग्नेय्यः ॥ विनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥ अनुवादवु— अनस्वंता ऎंब ई सूक्तवु ऎरडनॆय अनुवाकदल्लि हदिमूरनॆय सूक्तवु. इदरल्लि आरु ऋक्कुगळिरुववु. अनुक्रमणिकॆयल्लि—अनस्वंता षट् त्रैवृष्णपौरुकुत्सौ द्वौ त्र्यरुणत्रसदस्य राजानौ भारतश्चाश्वमेधोऽन्त्यास्ति सोऽनुष्टुभो नात्मात्मने दद्यादिति सर्वास्वत्रिं केचिदंतैंद्राग्नी (*anasvaṃtā ṣaṭ traivṛṣṇapaurukutsau dvā trairuṇatrasadasya rājān bhārataścāśvamedho 'ṃtyāstisro 'nuṣṭubho nātmātmane dadyāditi sarvāsvatriṃ kecidaṃtyaiṃdrāgnīti | trivṛṣṭasya putrastryaruṇaḥ purukutsasya putrastrasadasyurbharatasya putro 'śvamedha ete trayo 'pi rājānaḥ saṃbhūyāsya sūktasya ṛṣayaḥ | yadvā | atrireva ṛṣiḥ | adyāstisrastriṣṭubhaścaturthādyāsti sro 'nuṣṭubhaḥ | ṣaṣṭhīṃdrāgni devatyā śiṣṭāḥ paṃcāgneyyaḥ ‖ viniyogo laiṃgikaḥ ‖ anuvādavu— anasvaṃtā èṃba ī sūktavu èraḍanèya anuvākadalli hadimūranèya sūktavu. idaralli āru ṛkkugaḷiruvavu. anukramaṇikèyalli—anasvaṃtā ṣaṭ traivṛṣṇapaurukutsau dvau tryaruṇatrasadasya rājānau bhārataścāśvamedho 'ntyāsti so 'nuṣṭubho nātmātmane dadyāditi sarvāsvatriṃ kecidaṃtaiṃdrāgnī*). The seers of this sūkta are the three together: Tryaruṇa, son of King Trivṛṣṇa; Trasadasyu, son of King Purukutsa; and Aśvamedha, a prince, son of Bharata. Or else it can be said that Atri himself is the seer of this sūkta. The first three Ṛks of this sūkta are in the Triṣṭubh metre; the three from the fourth are in the Anuṣṭubh metre. The sixth Ṛk has Indra-Agni as its deity; the remaining five Ṛks have Agni as their deity. The use in ritual is by indication.

### Printed p. 143

Sūkta 27.

Maṇḍala 5, Anuvāka 2, Sūkta 27. Aṣṭaka 4, Adhyāya 1, Varga 21.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 6.

Seer: त्रैरुणस्त्रैवृष्ण स्त्रसदस्युश्च पौरुकुत्स अश्वमेधश्च भारत अत्रिर्वा ॥ (*trairuṇastraivṛṣṇa strasadasyuśca paurukutsa aśvamedhaśca bhārata atrirvā ‖*).

Deity: 1–5 Agni; 6 Indra-Agni.

Metre: 1–3 Triṣṭubh; 4–6 Anuṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अन॑स्वंता॒ सत्प॑तिर्मामहे मे॒ गावा॒ चेति॑ष्टो॒ असु॑रो  ॒॑
> मघोनः ।  
> त्रै॒वृ॒ष्णो अ॑ग्ने द॒शभिः॑ स॒हस्रै॒र्वैश्वा॑नर॒ त्रै॑रुणश्चिकेत ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनस्वंता । सत्पतिः । ममहे । मे । गावा । चेतिष्ठः । असुरः । मघोनः ।  
> त्रैवृष्णः । अग्ने । दशभिः । सहस्रैः । वैश्वानर । त्रिअरुणः । चिकेत ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्री राजर्षिणां दानमग्नेः पुरतोऽनेन सूक्तेनावर्णयत् । हे
> वैश्वानर विश्वेषां नराणां नेतरग्ने सत्पतिः सतां साधूनां पालयिता चेतिष्टो
> ज्ञातृतमोऽसुरो बलवान्मघोनो मघवा धनवान् ॥ प्रथमार्थे जसिः ॥ त्रैवृष्णस्त्रिवृष्णपुत्रस्र्यरुणस्त्र्यरुण इत्येतन्नामा राजर्षिरनस्वंतानस्वंतावनसा शकटेन
> संयुक्ता गावा गावावनड्वाहौ गवां हिरण्यादीनां दशभिः सहस्रैः सह मे मह्यं
> ममहे । ददौ ॥ महि दाने ॥ स राजर्षिश्चिकेत । सर्वैर्जनैरनेन दानेन ज्ञायते॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Atri described the gift of the royal seers in the presence of Agni by means of this sūkta. O Vaiśvānara, leader of all men, Agni, you are *satpati*, the protector of the good and the righteous; *cetiṣṭha*, the most knowing; *asura*, the powerful; and *maghonaḥ*, one possessing wealth. Tryaruṇa, the royal seer of that name, son of Trivṛṣṇa, gave (*mamahe*) to me the two oxen *anasvantā* (that is, joined with a cart, *anas*) together with ten thousands of cattle, gold and the like. That royal seer is known (*ciketa*) to all people by this gift. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वैश्वानर (*vaiśvānara*)—O leader of all men
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सत्पतिः (*satpatiḥ*)—protector of the good
- चेतिष्ठः (*cetiṣṭhaḥ*)—the best among the discerning
- असुरः (*asuraḥ*)—endowed with strength
- मघोनः (*maghonaḥ*)—possessing wealth

### Printed p. 144

- त्रैवृष्णः (*traivṛṣṇaḥ*)—son of Trivṛṣṇa
- त्र्यरुणः (*tryaruṇaḥ*)—the royal seer named Tryaruṇa
- मे (*me*)—to me
- अनस्वंता (*anasvaṃtā*)—those joined with a cart
- दशभिः सहस्रैः (*daśabhiḥ sahasraiḥ*)—those joined with ten thousand pieces of gold
- गावा (*gāvā*)—the pair of cattle
- ममहे (*mamahe*)—gave (and therefore)
- चिकेत (*ciketa*)—he has become known and famous to all

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, in the form of Vaiśvānara, protector of the good, best among the discerning, endowed with strength and wealth: the royal seer named Tryaruṇa, son of Trivṛṣṇa, gave me a pair of cattle (two oxen) joined with a cart and with ten thousand pieces of gold. For that very reason he has become known and famous to all.

##### English translation
Agni, who are the protector of the good, most wise, powerful, and opulent; Tryaruna, the son of Trivrishna, has become renowned, Vaishwanara, in that he has bestowed upon me a pair of cattle with a waggon, and with ten thousands of treasure.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो मे॑ श॒ता च॑ विंश॒तिं च॒ गोनां॒ हरी॑ च यु॒क्ता  
> सु॒धुरा॒ ददा॑ति ।  
> वैश्वा॑नर॒ सुष्टु॑तो वावृधा॒नोऽग्ने॒ यच्छ॒ त्र्य॑रुणाय॒  ॑
> शर्म ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । मे । शता । च । विंशतिं । च । गोनां । हरी इति । च । युक्ता । सुधुरा ।  
> ददाति ।  
> वैश्वानर । सुस्तुतः । ववृधानः । अग्ने । यच्छ । त्रिअरुणाय । शर्म ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 145

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्त्र्यरुणः शता च शतानि सुवर्णानां गोनां गवां विंशतिं च युक्ता
> रथेन युक्ता सुधुरा सुधुरौ सुष्टु धुरं वहंतौ हरी अश्वा च मे मह्यं ददाति
> अददात् हे वैश्वानराग्ने सुष्टुतोऽस्माभिः सम्यक्सुतो ववृधानो हविर्भिर्वर्धमानस्त्वं तस्मै त्रैरुणाय शर्म सुखं यच्छ । प्रयच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa explains that Tryaruṇa gives me hundreds of pieces of gold, twenty of cattle (*gonām* means *gavām*), and two horses (*harī*), which are yoked to a chariot and bear the yoke well (*sudhurā*). He then addresses Agni Vaiśvānara, well praised by us (*suṣṭuta*) **NOTE** and growing by the oblations (*vavṛdhāna*): bestow (*yaccha*, that is, *prayaccha*) happiness (*śarma*) on that Tryaruṇa.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वैश्वानर (*vaiśvānara*)—who are of the form of Vaiśvānara
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सुष्टुतः (*suṣṭutaḥ*)—well praised by us
- ववृधानः (*vavṛdhānaḥ*)—and made to grow by oblations, you who are so
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Tryaruṇa who
- मे (*me*)—to me
- शता च (*śatā ca*)—and a hundred pieces of gold
- गोनां विंशतिं (*gonāṃ viṃśatiṃ*)—twenty cows
- सुधरा (*sudharā*)—those fit to bear a load
- युक्ता (*yuktā*)—and yoked to a chariot
- हरी च (*harī ca*)—and two horses
- ददाति (*dadāti*)—gave, to such a one
- त्रैरुणाय (*trairuṇāya*)—to the royal seer named Tryaruṇa
- शर्म (*śarma*)—happiness
- यच्छ (*yaccha*)—bestow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who are of the form of Vaiśvānara, you who are well praised by us and made to grow by oblations: bring happiness to the royal seer named Tryaruṇa, who has given me a hundred pieces of gold, twenty cows, and two horses fit to bear a load and yoked to a chariot.

##### English translation
Agni, Vaishwanara, you are deservedly praised and exalted ( by us ); bestow happiness upon Tryaruna, who gives me hundreds (of Suvarnas), twenty cattle, and a pair of burden-bearing horses.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा ते॑ अग्ने सुम॒तिं च॑का॒नो नवि॑ष्ठाय नव॒मं त्र॒सद॑स्युः।  
> यो मे॒ गिर॑स्तुविजा॒तस्य॑ पू॒र्वीर्यु॒क्तेना॒भि त्र्य॑रुणो  ॒॑
> गृणाति ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 146

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । ते । अग्ने । सुमतिं । चकानः । नविष्ठाय । नवमं । त्रसदस्युः ।  
> यः । मे । गिरः । तुन्विजातस्य । पूर्वीः । युक्तेन । अभि । त्रिऽरुणः । गृणाति ॥३॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्त्ररुणस्तु विजातस्य बह्वपत्यस्य मे पूर्विर्बह्वीर्गिरः स्तुतीः श्रुत्वा
> प्रीतः सन् युक्तेनाभियुक्तेन मनसाभि गृणाति इदं गृहाणेदं गृहाणेति तथा
> मां ब्रवीति एवैवं हे अग्ने नविष्ठायात्यंतं स्तुत्याय ते तुभ्यं नवमं
> नवतमां सुमतिं स्तुतिं चकानः कामयमानस्रसदस्युरपीदं गृहाणेदं गृहाणेति
> मां प्रार्थितवान् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Tryaruṇa, hearing the many praises (*girah*) of me, who have many children (*tuvijāta*), is pleased; with a concentrated mind (*yuktena*, that is, *abhiyuktena*) he says to me again and again, "Take this, take this." In the same way, O Agni, who are exceedingly worthy of praise (*naviṣṭha*), Trasadasyu too, desiring a praise (*sumati*) that is newest (*navama*, that is, *navatama*) for you, has begged me, "Take this, take this."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यः त्र्यरुणः (*yaḥ tryaruṇaḥ*)—the Tryaruṇa who
- तुविजातस्य (*tuvijātasya*)—having many sons
- मे (*me*)—by me composed
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—and many
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—(praising you), by the praises (being pleased)
- युक्तेन (*yuktena*)—with a mind full of devotion
- अभि गृणाति (*abhi gṛṇāti*)—says "Accept these gifts"
- एव (*eva*)—in that very way
- नविष्ठाय (*naviṣṭhāya*)—who are worthy of exceeding praise
- ते (*te*)—with you in view (by me)
- नवमः (*navamaḥ*)—newly composed
- सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)—excellent praise
- चकानः (*cakānaḥ*)—desiring
- त्रसदस्युः (*trasadasyuḥ*)—Trasadasyu too (has begged me to accept these gifts)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, Tryaruṇa was gratified by the excellent praises composed and recited with you in view, so that your favour might be gained for Tryaruṇa; and he gave me, who have many sons, the most excellent gifts with devotion. In the same way Trasadasyu too, desiring that I compose an excellent praise with you in view for the gaining of your favour for himself, has begged me to accept excellent gifts.

##### English translation
As Tryaruna, pleased by the eulogies of me who have many children, presses with earnest (mind, gifts upon me), so does Trasadasvu. desirous, Agni, of your valued favour through your exceeding praise.

### Printed p. 147

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो म॒ इति॑ प्र॒वोच॒त्यश्व॑मेधाय सू॒रये॑ ।  
> दद॑द्व॒चा स॒निं य॒ते दद॑न्मे॒धामृ॑ताय॒ते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । मे । इति । प्रवोचति । अश्वमेधाय । सूरये ।  
> ददत् । ऋचा । सनिं । यते । ददत् । मेधां । ऋत्ययते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो भिक्षमाणोऽर्थी सूरये प्रेरकाय धनानां दात्रे वास्मा अश्वमेधाय राजर्षये मे मह्यं देहीति प्रवोचति प्रब्रवीति । योऽश्वमेधं धनानि
> भिक्षत इत्यर्थः । ऋचाग्नेः स्तोत्रेण सह यत आत्मनः समीपं गच्छते तस्मा अर्थिनेऽश्वमेधः सनिं धनं ददत् । ददाति । हे अग्ने ऋतायते यज्ञमिच्छते तस्मा
> अश्वमेधाय मेधां यज्ञविषयां प्रज्ञां ददत् । देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The petitioner who begs says to Aśvamedha, the royal seer, who is *sūri*, that is, the impeller and giver of wealth, "Give to me" (*pravocati*); that is, he begs wealth from Aśvamedha. Aśvamedha gives wealth (*sani*) to that petitioner who comes (*yate*) to him, that is, goes to his presence, with a *ṛc*, a praise of Agni. O Agni, give to that Aśvamedha, who desires the sacrifice (*ṛtāyate*), insight (*medhā*), that is, wisdom concerning the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) यः (*yaḥ*)—the petitioner who
- सूरये (*sūraye*)—who is a giver of wealth
- अश्वमेधाय (*aśvamedhāya*)—to the royal seer named Aśvamedha
- मे (*me*)—to me (give wealth)
- इति (*iti*)—thus
- प्रवोचति (*pravocati*)—begs; such a one
- ऋचा (*ṛcā*)—together with a praise (that extols Agni)
- यते (*yate*)—to him who goes to him
- सनिं (*saniṃ*)—wealth
- ददत् (*dadat*)—he gives
- ऋतायते (*ṛtāyate*)—to that Aśvamedha, who desires the sacrifice
- मेधां (*medhāṃ*)—good understanding
- ददत् (*dadat*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the petitioner who approaches the royal seer named Aśvamedha, the giver of wealth, and begs wealth of him together with a praise that wins your favour and extols you, to such a man he gives wealth, in the faith that good will come to him from that praise. Give good understanding to that Aśvamedha, who desires the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 148

##### English translation
When Ashwamedha gives to him who solicits of him as a benefactor, saying (bestow wealth) upon me, and comes (to him) with a verse (in your praise), do you, Agni, grant intelligence to (the Raja) who wishes to offer sacrifice (to you).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॑ मा परु॒षाः श॒तमु॑द्ध॒र्षयं॑त्यु॒क्षणः॑ ।  
> अश्व॑मेधस्य॒ दानाः॒ सोमा॑ इव॒ त्र्या॑शिरः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । मा । परुषाः । शतं । उत्सहर्षयंति । उक्षणः ।  
> अश्वमेधस्य । दानाः । सोमाःइव । त्रिआशिरः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्य येनाश्वमेधस्याश्वमेधेन दाना दत्ताः परुषाः कामानां पूरकाः
> शतमुक्षण उक्षाणो बलीवर्दा मा मामुद्धर्षयंति उत्कर्षेण हर्षयंति हे
> अग्ने त उक्षाणस्त्वाशिरः । दधिसक्तुपयोरूपास्तिस्त्र आशिरोऽधिश्रयणसाधनभूता
> येषां ते त्वाशिरः । सोमा इव तव प्रीणनाय भवंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The hundred bulls (*ukṣaṇaḥ*, that is, oxen) given by Aśvamedha as gifts, which fulfil desires (*paruṣāḥ*), delight me exceedingly. O Agni, may those bulls, which have the three mixtures (*āśir*) of curds, barley meal and milk as the means of cooking **NOTE**, be, like Soma, for your gratification.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) यस्य (*yasya*)—whose
- अश्वमेधस्य (*aśvamedhasya*)—by Aśvamedha
- दानाः (*dānāḥ*)—given
- परुषाः (*paruṣāḥ*)—and fulfilling desires
- शतं उक्षणः (*śataṃ ukṣaṇaḥ*)—a hundred bulls
- मा (*mā*)—me
- त्रशिरः (*traśiraḥ*)—of the form of the three mixtures, curds and the rest
- सोमा इव (*somā iva*)—like Soma juices
- उद्धर्षयंति (*uddharṣayaṃti*)—gratify (may they also serve for your gratification)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, just as the hundred bulls given by this same Aśvamedha, which fulfil desires, gratify me like Soma juices of the three mixtures of curds and the rest, so may the oblations given by him bring gratification to you.

### Printed p. 149

##### English translation
Whose hundred robust oxen yield me delight, as the triple-mixed Soma, the offering of Ashwamedha (gratifies you).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रा॑ग्नी शत॒दान्यश्व॑मेधे सु॒वीर्यं॑ ।  
> क्ष॒त्रं धा॑रयतं बृ॒हद्दि॒वि सूर्य॑मिना॒जरं॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्राग्नी इति । शतदान्नि । अश्वमेधे । सुवीर्यं ।  
> क्षत्रं । धारयतं । बृहत् । दिवि । सूर्यंइव । अजरं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्राग्नी शतदान्नि । शतमपरिमितमर्थिभ्यो धनं ददातीति शतदावा ।
> तस्मिन्नश्वमेधे राजर्षौ सुवीर्यं शोभनवीर्यसहितं बृहन्महदजरं जरारहितं
> क्षत्रं धनं धारयतं । निधारयतं । कथमिव । दिव्यंतरिक्षे सूर्यमिव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra and Agni, *śatadānni* means in him who gives to petitioners a hundred, that is, immeasurable, wealth (*śatadāvā*). In that Aśvamedha, the royal seer, establish (*dhārayatam*, that is, *nidhārayatam*) *kṣatra*, that is, wealth, endowed with good heroism (*suvīrya*), great and free from old age (*ajara*). How? As the sun in the sky (*divi*, that is, in the atmosphere).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्राग्नी (*iṃdrāgnī*)—O gods Indra and Agni
- शतदान्नि (*śatadānni*)—who gives wealth without measure
- अश्वमेधे (*aśvamedhe*)—in the royal seer named Aśvamedha
- सुवीर‍्यं (*suvīryaṃ*)—endowed with excellent strength
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundant
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the atmosphere
- सूर‍्यं इव (*sūryaṃ iva*)—shining like the sun
- अजरं (*ajaraṃ*)—imperishable
- क्षत्रं (*kṣatraṃ*)—wealth
- धारयतं (*dhārayataṃ*)—fill (it)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra and Agni, fill the royal seer named Aśvamedha, who gives wealth without measure, with wealth that is endowed with excellent strength, abundant, shining like the sun in the atmosphere, and everlasting.

### Printed p. 150

##### English translation
Indra and Agni, bestow upon the munificent Ashwamedha infinite wealth with excellent posterity, undecaying as the sun in heaven.

## Sūkta 28 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-eighth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> समिद्धो अग्निरिति षडृचं चतुर्दशं सूक्तं । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका ।
> समिद्धो विश्ववारात्रेयी त्रिष्टुब्बगती त्रिष्टुबनुष्टुब्गायत्राविति । अत्रिगोत्रोत्पन्ना विश्ववारानामिकास्य सूक्तस्य ऋषिः । आद्यातृतीये त्रिष्टुभौ द्वितीया
> जगती चतुर्थ्यनुष्टुप् अंत्ये गायत्रौ । अग्निर्देवता ॥ सूक्तविनियोगो
> लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning *samiddho agniḥ* has six Ṛks and is the fourteenth sūkta. Sāyaṇa cites the Anukramaṇikā for its seer, metres and deity. The seer of this sūkta is the woman named Viśvavārā, born in the lineage of Atri. The first and third Ṛks are in Triṣṭubh, the second in Jagatī, the fourth in Anuṣṭubh, and the last two in Gāyatrī. Agni is the deity. The use of the sūkta in ritual is by indication (*laiṅgika*).

The sūkta beginning *samiddho agniḥ* is the fourteenth sūkta in the second Anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: समिद्धो विश्ववारात्रेयी त्रिष्टुब्बगती त्रिष्टुबनुष्टुब्गायत् (*samiddho viśvavārātreyī triṣṭubbagatī triṣṭubanuṣṭubgāyat*). For this sūkta the woman named Viśvavārā, born in the lineage of Atri, is the deity **NOTE**; Agni is the deity. The first and third Ṛks of this sūkta are in the Triṣṭubh metre; the second Ṛk is in Jagatī; the fourth Ṛk is in Anuṣṭubh; the last two Ṛks are in Gāyatrī. The use in ritual is by indication.

Sūkta 28.

Maṇḍala 5, Anuvāka 2, Sūkta 28. Aṣṭaka 4, Adhyāya 1, Varga 22.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 6.

Seer: विश्ववारात्रेयि (*viśvavārātreyi*).

Deity: Agni.

Metre: 1–3 Triṣṭubh; 2 Jagatī; 4 Anuṣṭubh; 5–6 Gāyatrī. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 151

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> समि॑द्दो अ॒ग्निर्दि॒वि शो॒चिर॑श्रेत्प्र॒त्यज्जु॒षस॑मुर्वि॒या वि भा॑ति ।  
> एति॒ प्राची॑ वि॒श्ववा॑रा॒ नमो॑भिर्दे॒वा ईळा॑ना ह॒विषा॑  ॒॑
> घृताची ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइद्दः । अग्निः । दिवि । शोचिः । अश्रेत् । प्रत्यज् । उषसं । उर्विया । वि । भाति ।  
> एति । प्राची । विश्वनारा । नमःभिः । देवान् । ईळाना । हविषा । घृताची ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> समिद्धः सम्यग्ग्रीप्तोऽग्निर्दिवि द्योतमानेऽंतरिक्षे शोचिस्तेजोऽश्रेत् ।
> श्रयति । तथोषसं प्रत्यज् उषसमभिमुखः सन् उर्वियोरु विस्तीर्णं वि भाति ।
> विशेषेण भ्राजते । नमोभिः स्तोत्रैर्देवानिंद्रादीनीळाना स्तुवती हविषा
> पुरोडाशादिलक्षणेन युक्तया धृताची धृताच्या सुचा सहिता विश्ववारा सर्वमपि
> पापरूपं शत्रुं वारयित्र्येतन्नामिका प्राचि प्राङ्मुखी सत्येति । एवंभूतमग्निं
> प्रतिगच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, fully kindled, spreads (*aśret*, that is, *śrayati*) his lustre in the shining atmosphere; likewise, facing the dawn, he shines widely (*urviyā*) and brilliantly. Viśvavārā, so named because she keeps off every sinful enemy, faces east (*prācī*, that is, *prāṅmukhī*); she praises the gods, Indra and the others, with praises (*namobhiḥ*), and, provided with oblations such as the *puroḍāśa* and with the ghee-ladle (*dhṛtācī*), goes towards Agni who is thus.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- समिद्धः (*samiddhaḥ*)—fully kindled
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the shining atmosphere
- शोचिः (*śociḥ*)—his lustre
- अश्रेत् (*aśret*)—spreads
- उषसं प्रत्यज् (*uṣasaṃ pratyaj*)—facing the dawn
- उर्विया (*urviyā*)—widely
- वि भाति (*vi bhāti*)—shines
- नमोभीः (*namobhīḥ*)—with praises
- देवान् (*devān*)—the gods, Indra and the rest
- ईळाना (*īḷānā*)—praising
- हविषा (*haviṣā*)—furnished with oblations
- धृताची (*dhṛtācī*)—holding the ladle
- विश्ववारा (*viśvavārā*)—the seer named Viśvavārā
- प्राची (*prācī*)—facing east (facing Agni)
- एति (*eti*)—approaches Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, fully kindled, spreads his lustre in the shining atmosphere. Facing the dawn, he shines widely. Praising Indra and the other gods with praises, and holding the ladle furnished with oblations, the seer named Viśvavārā goes towards Agni, facing him.

### Printed p. 152

##### English translation
Agni, when kindled, spreads lustre through the firmament, and shines in widely the presence of the dawn: Vishwavara, facing the east, glorifying the gods with praises, and bearing the ladle with the oblation, proceeds (to the sacred fire).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒मि॒ध्यमा॑नो अ॒मृत॑स्य राजसि ह॒विष्कृ॒ण्वंतं॑ सचसे स्व॒स्तये॑ ।  
> विश्वं॒ स ध॑त्ते॒ द्रवि॑णं॒ यमिन्व॑ स्याति॒थ्यम॑ग्ने॒ नि च॑ धत्त॒ इत्पु॒रः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइध्यमानः । अमृतस्य । राजसि । हविः । कृण्वंतं । सचसे । स्वस्तये ।  
> विश्वं । सः । धत्ते । द्रविणं । यं । इन्वसि । आतिथ्यं । अग्ने । नि । च । धत्ते । इत् ।  
> पुरः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने समिध्यमानः सम्यगिध्यमानस्त्वममृतस्योदकस्य राजसि । ईशिषे ।
> तथा हविष्कृण्वंतं पुरोडाशादिहविष्कर्तारं यजमानं स्वस्तयेऽविनाशाय सचसे ।
> सेवसे । किंच यं यजमानमिन्वसि गच्छसि स यजमानो विश्वं समस्तं द्रविणं
> पश्वादिलक्षणं धनं धत्ते । धारयति । अपि च हे अग्ने आतिथ्यमतिथिरूपस्य तव योग्यं
> हविः पुर इत् तव पुरस्तादेव नि धत्ते च । स्थापयति च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, being well kindled, you rule (*rājasi*, that is, *īśiṣe*) over *amṛta*, that is, water. You also attend (*sacase*, that is, *sevase*) the sacrificer who makes oblations such as the *puroḍāśa*, for his welfare (*svastaye*), that is, for his freedom from destruction. Moreover, the sacrificer to whom you come (*invasi*, that is, *gacchasi*) holds all wealth (*dravinam*) of the kind that consists of cattle and the like. And, O Agni, he sets before you, as the guest that you are, the oblation fitting for your hospitality (*ātithya*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) समिध्यमानः (*samidhyamānaḥ*)—when you are kindled
- अमृतस्य (*amṛtasya*)—of water
- राजसि (*rājasi*)—you are lord
- हविष्कृण्वंतं (*haviṣkṛṇvaṃtaṃ*)—the sacrificer who offers the oblation
- स्वस्तये (*svastaye*)—to bring him welfare
- सचसे (*sacase*)—you attend
- यं (*yaṃ*)—whichever sacrificer
- इन्वसि (*invasi*)—you favour and approach
- सः (*saḥ*)—that sacrificer
- विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)—all
- द्रविणं (*draviṇaṃ*)—wealth
- धत्ते (*dhatte*)—obtains
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- आतिथ्यं (*ātithyaṃ*)—the oblation fit for your worship as guest
- पुर इत् (*pura it*)—right in front of you
- नि धत्ते (*ni dhatte*)—he places

### Printed p. 153

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, when you are kindled you are lord of all the water. You attend the sacrificer who makes the oblation, so that welfare may come to him. The sacrificer whom you favour and attend obtains all wealth. O Agni, he places the oblation fit for your worship as guest right in front of you.

##### English translation
When about to be kindled, Agni, you rule over ambrosial (water) : you are present with the offerer of the oblation for his welfare: he to whom you repair acquires universal wealth ; he places before you, Agni, the dues of hospitality.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ शर्ध॑ मह॒ते सौभ॑गाय॒ तव॑ द्यु॒म्नान्यु॑त्त॒मानि॑ संतु ।  
> सं जा॑स्प॒त्यं सु॒यम॒मा कृ॑णुष्व शत्रूय॒ताम॒भि ति॑ष्ठा॒  ॑
> महांसि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । शर्ध । महते । सौभगाय । तव । द्युम्नानि । उत्तमानि । संतु ।  
> सं । जाःपत्यं । सुयमं । आ । कृणुष्ट । शत्रुयतां । अभि । तिष्ठ । महांसि ॥३॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पवित्रेष्ट्यां स्विष्टकृतोऽग्ने शर्धॆति याज्या । सूत्र्यते हि । जुष्टो दमूना
> अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये । आ. २. १२ । इति ॥ साकमेधेषु
> मरुद्भ्यः क्रीडिभ्यः पुरोडाशं सप्तकपालमित्यत्राप्येष्टैव स्विष्टकृतो याज्या ।
> सूत्रितं च । जुष्टो दमूना अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये ।
> आ. २. १८. । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Pavitreṣṭi, the verse beginning *agne śardha* is the yājyā of the Sviṣṭakṛt offering. It is laid down in the sūtra: जुष्टो दमूना अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये (*juṣṭo damūnā agne śardha mahate saubhagāyeti saṃyājye*) (Ā. 2. 12). In the Sākamedha offerings too, at the offering "to the sporting Maruts, a *puroḍāśa* on seven potsherds", the Sviṣṭakṛt yājyā is this same verse, and the sūtra says so: जुष्टो दमूना अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये (*juṣṭo damūnā agne śardha mahate saubhagāyeti saṃyājye*) (Ā. 2. 18).

### Printed p. 154

> हे अग्ने त्वं महते प्रभूताय सौभगायास्माकं शोभनधनत्वाय शर्ध ।
> शत्रून् सहस्व । तथा तव संबंधीनि द्युम्नानि धनानि तेजांसि वा उत्तमान्त्युत्कृष्टानि संतु । भवंतु । किंच हे अग्ने सं जास्पत्यं । जा जाया च पतिश्च जायापती ।
> तयोः कर्म जास्पत्यं । तत्सुयमं सुष्टु नियमनोपेतं । अन्नोन्यसंश्लिष्टमित्यर्थः । समा कृणुष्व । सम्यक्कुरुष्व । अपि च शत्रूयतां शत्रुमात्मन इच्छतां
> सपत्नानां महांसि तेजांस्यभि तिष्ठ । आक्रमस्व ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, for our great (*mahate*, that is, abundant) good fortune (*saubhaga*, that is, good wealth), *śardha*: overcome the enemies. Let your *dyumnāni*, that is, riches or splendours, be excellent. Moreover, O Agni, make *jāspatya* (*jā* is wife, and the husband too; the pair is *jāyāpatī*) well-regulated, that is, closely joined to one another; make it good. And overcome (*abhi tiṣṭha*, that is, attack) the energies of the rivals who wish for themselves an enemy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- महते (*mahate*)—exceeding
- सौभगाय (*saubhagāya*)—so that blessed wealth may come to us
- शर्ध (*śardha*)—check our enemies
- तव (*tava*)—belonging to you
- द्युम्नानि (*dyumnāni*)—riches
- उत्तमानि (*uttamāni*)—excellent
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—let them be
- जास्पत्यं (*jāspatyaṃ*)—the good rites that point to the mutual relation of husband and wife
- सुयमं (*suyamaṃ*)—joined to one another, as if under one rule
- सं आकृणुष्ट्व (*saṃ ākṛṇuṣṭva*)—perfect
- शत्रूयतां (*śatrūyatāṃ*)—of those who wish us harm
- महांसि (*mahāṃsi*)—the energies
- अभि तिष्ठ (*abhi tiṣṭha*)—check and stand against

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, check our enemies, so that blessed wealth may come to us in abundance. Let your riches be excellent. Perfect the good rites that point to the mutual relation of husband and wife, joined to one another as if under one rule. Check and overcome the energies of those who wish us harm.

##### English translation
Repress, Agni, (our foes to ensure our) exceeding prosperity : may your riches ever be excellent : preserve in concord the relation of man and wife, and overpower the energies of the hostile.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Pavitreṣṭi, this verse beginning अग्ने शर्ध (*agne śardha*) is to be recited as the yājyā mantra at the Sviṣṭakṛt offering; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, जुष्टो दमूना अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये (*juṣṭo damūnā agne śardha mahate saubhagāyeti saṃyājye*) (Ā. 2-12). Again, in the Sākamedha sacrifices, when the offering is made with the words मरुद्भ्यः क्रीडिभ्यः पुरोडाशं सप्तकपालं (*marudbhyaḥ krīḍibhyaḥ puroḍāśaṃ saptakapālaṃ*), this verse is to be recited as the yājyā mantra at the Sviṣṭakṛt offering; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, जुष्टो दमूना अग्ने शर्ध महते सौभगायेति संयाज्ये (*juṣṭo damūnā agne śardha mahate saubhagāyeti saṃyājye*) (Ā. 2-18).

### Printed p. 155

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> समि॑द्धस्य॒ प्रम॑ह॒सोऽग्ने॒ वंदे॒ तव॒ श्रियं॑ ।  
> वृ॒ष॒भो द्यु॒म्नवा॑ असि॒ सम॑ध्व॒रेष्वि॑ध्यसे ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइद्धस्य । प्रमहसः । अग्ने । वंदे । तव । श्रियं ।  
> वृषभः । द्युम्नवान् । असि । सं । अध्वरेषु । इध्यसे ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने समिद्धस्य प्रवृद्धस्य प्रमहसः प्रकृष्टतेजसस्तव संबंधिनीं श्रियं
> दीप्तिं वंदे । .अहं यजमानः सौमि । वृषभः कामानां वर्षिता त्वं द्युम्नवानसि । धनवान्भवसि । अध्वरेषु यज्ञेषु समिध्यसे । सम्यग्दीप्यसे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, I, the sacrificer, praise (*stauti*) **NOTE** the glory (*śrī*), that is, the brightness, that belongs to you, who are kindled, that is, grown strong, of excellent splendour (*pramahas*). You are the showerer (*vṛṣabha*) of desires and are wealthy (*dyumnavān*). In the sacrifices (*adhvareṣu*) you are well kindled.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- समिद्धस्य (*samiddhasya*)—who are fully kindled
- प्र महसः (*pra mahasaḥ*)—and who have most excellent splendour
- तव (*tava*)—your
- श्रियं (*śriyaṃ*)—splendour
- वंदे (*vaṃde*)—I bow to
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—you, the showerer of desires
- द्युम्नवान् (*dyumnavān*)—wealthy with brightness
- आसि (*āsi*)—are
- अध्वरेषु (*adhvareṣu*)—in the sacrifices
- सं इध्यसे (*saṃ idhyase*)—you are well kindled

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, I bow to the splendour of you, who are fully kindled and have most excellent splendour. You, the showerer of desires, are wealthy with brightness. In the sacrifices you are well kindled.

##### English translation
I praise the glory, Agni, of you when kindled and blazing fiercely : you are the affluent showerer ( of benefits ), you are fitly lighted at sacrifices.

### Printed p. 156

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> समु॑द्धो अग्न आहुत दे॒वान्य॑क्षि स्वध्वर ।  
> त्वं हि ह॑व्य॒वाळसि॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइद्धः । अग्ने । आहुत । देवान् । यक्षि । सुअध्वर ।  
> त्वं । हि । हव्यनाट् । असि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दर्शपूर्णमासयोः समिद्धो अग्न आहुतेति द्वे सामिधेन्न्यौ । सूत्रितं
> च । समिद्धो अग्न आहुतेति द्वे । आ. १-२ । इति ॥
> हे आहुत यजमानैरा समंताद्भुत हे स्वध्वर शोभनयज्योपेत हे
> अग्ने समिद्धः सम्यग्दीप्तस्त्वं देवान्दोतमानानिंद्रादीन्यक्षि । यजस्व । हि यस्मात्कारणात् हे अग्ने त्वं हव्यनाडसि हव्यानां वोढा भवसि । अतः कारणाद्देवान्यजस्वेति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the new-moon and full-moon sacrifices, the two verses beginning *samiddho agna āhuta* are the two Sāmidhenī verses. The sūtra says so: समिद्धो अग्न आहुतेति द्वे (*samiddho agna āhuteti dve*) (Ā. 1-2). O invoked one (*āhuta*), called from every side by the sacrificers; O one of good sacrifice (*svadhvara*); O Agni, being fully kindled, worship (*yakṣi*, that is, *yajasva*) the gods, Indra and the other shining ones. For you are the bearer of the oblations (*havyavāṭ*, that is, *havyānāṃ voḍhā*); for that reason, the connection is, worship the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आहुत (*āhuta*)—called by the sacrificers
- स्वध्वर (*svadhvara*)—having an excellent sacrifice
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- समिद्धः (*samiddhaḥ*)—fully kindled
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- देवान् (*devān*)—for the gods
- यक्षि (*yakṣi*)—perform the sacrifice
- हि (*hi*)—because
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- हव्यवाट् (*havyavāṭ*)—the bearer of the oblation
- असि (*asi*)—are

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, called by the sacrificers and having an excellent sacrifice, you are the bearer of the oblation to the gods. Therefore, as soon as you are kindled by the sacrificer, perform the sacrifice for the gods.

##### English translation
Agni, you are kindled and invoked ; worship the gods at the holy rite, for you are the bearer of the oblation.

### Printed p. 157

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Darśapūrṇamāsa sacrifices, this Ṛk beginning समिद्दो अग्न आहुत (*samiddo agna āhuta*) and the following Ṛk are to be used as sāmidhenī mantras; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, समिद्दो अग्न आहुतेति द्वे (*samiddo agna āhuteti dve*) (Ā. Sū. 1-2).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ जु॑होता दुव॒स्यता॒ग्निं प्र॑य॒त्य॑ध्व॒रे ।  
> वृ॒णी॒ध्वं ह॑व्य॒वाह॑नं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । जुहोत । दुवस्यत । अग्निं । प्रयति । अध्वरे ।  
> वृणीध्वं । हव्यवाहनं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋत्विजो यूयमध्वरेऽस्मदीययागे प्रयति प्रवृत्ते सति हव्यवाहनं
> हविषां वोढारमेतन्नामकमग्निमा जुहोत । आ समंताज्जु होत । तथा दुवस्यत ।
> परिचरत । वृणीध्वं । संभजध्वं च हव्यवाहनं । हव्यवाहननामकस्यॆवाग्नेर्देवतासंबंधो यजमानैर्वर्णनीयत्वं च तैत्तिरीये स्पष्टमाम्मातं । त्रयो वा
> अग्नयो हव्यवाहनो देवानां कव्यवाहनः पितृणां सहरक्षा असुराणां । त
> एतर्हाशंसंते मां वरिष्यते मामिति वृणीध्वं हव्यवाहनमित्याह य एव देवानां
> तं वृणीते ॥ तै. सं. २-५-८-६ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O priests, when our sacrifice has begun, make offering to Agni, the bearer of the oblations (हव्यवाहनं (*havyavāhanaṃ*)), from every side; likewise serve him, and choose him. That Agni who is named Havyavāhana is connected with the gods, and that he is to be chosen by the sacrificers, is stated plainly in the Taittirīya (Tai. Saṃ. 2-5-8-6): there are three Agnis, Havyavāhana of the gods, Kavyavāhana of the fathers, and Sahaṃrakṣas of the Asuras. These now hope, "he will choose me, me"; by saying "choose Havyavāhana" he chooses the one who belongs to the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अध्वरे (*adhvare*)—(O priests,) our sacrifice
- प्रयति (*prayati*)—as soon as it has begun
- हव्यवाहनं (*havyavāhanaṃ*)—who carries the oblation
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—to Agni
- आ जुहोत (*ā juhota*)—offer the oblation
- दुवस्यत (*duvasyata*)—worship
- वृणीध्वं (*vṛṇīdhvaṃ*)—choose (him as the messenger who brings your oblation to the gods)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, as soon as our sacrifice has begun, offer the oblation to Agni, who carries the oblation. Worship him and choose him as the messenger who brings your oblation to the gods.

### Printed p. 158

##### English translation
Offer worship and adoration to Agni, when the sacrifice is solemnized ; select the bearer of the oblation to the gods.

## Sūkta 29 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-ninth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> त्र्ययमेति पंचदशर्चं पंचदशं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । त्र्ययमा
> पंचोना गौरिवीतिः शाक्त्य ऐंद्रमुशना यदौशनसो वा पाद इति । शक्तिगोत्रोत्पन्नो गौरिवीतिर्नाम ऋषिः । अनुक्तत्वात्रिष्टुप् । इंद्रो देवता मंडलादिपरिभाषया निवृत्तत्वात् । उशना यत्सहस्यैरिति पाद औशनस ऐंद्रो वा ॥ अभिप्लविके
> तृतीयेऽहनि मरुत्वतीय एतन्निविद्वानं । सूत्रितं च । तृतीयस्य त्र्ययमा यो
> जात एवेति मध्यंदिनः ॥ आ. ७-७ । इति॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning त्र्ययमेति (*tryayameti*) has fifteen Ṛks and is the fifteenth sūkta. Sāyaṇa quotes the Anukramaṇikā (त्र्ययमा पंचोना गौरिवीतिः शाक्त्य ऐंद्रमुशना यदौशनसो वा पाद इति (*tryayamā paṃconā gaurivītiḥ śāktya aiṃdramuśanā yadauśanaso vā pāda iti*)) **NOTE**. The seer is named Gauriviti, born in the lineage of Śakti. The metre is Triṣṭubh, since no other is stated. The deity is Indra, since the general rule of the Maṇḍala and the like settles the matter. The pāda beginning उशना यत्सहस्यैरिति (*uśanā yatsahasyairiti*) has Auśana (Uśanā) or Indra as its deity. At the Abhiplava rite, on the third day, in the Marutvatīya recitation, this sūkta serves as the nivid-sūkta, and the sūtra says so: तृतीयस्य त्र्ययमा यो जात एवेति मध्यंदिनः ॥ (*tṛtīyasya tryayamā yo jāta eveti madhyaṃdinaḥ ‖*) (Ā. Sū. 7-7).

Translation (Anuvāda): This sūkta, beginning *tryaryamā*, is the fifteenth sūkta in the second Anuvāka. It has fifteen Ṛks. The Anukramaṇikā says: त्र्यर्यमा पंचोना गौरिवीतिः शाक्त्य ऐंद्रमुशना यदौशनसो वा पादः (*tryaryamā paṃconā gaurivītiḥ śāktya aiṃdramuśanā yadauśanaso vā pādaḥ*). The seer of this sūkta is Gauriviti, born in the lineage of Śakti; the metre is Triṣṭubh; the deity is Indra; for the first pāda of the ninth Ṛk, उशना यत्सहस्यॆः (*uśanā yatsahasyèḥ*), the deity is Indra or Uśanā. At the Abhiplava sacrifice, on the third day, when the Marutvatīya śastra mantras are recited, this sūkta is to be recited as the nivid-sūkta; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, त्र्यर्यमा यो जात एवेति मध्यंदिनः (*tryaryamā yo jāta eveti madhyaṃdinaḥ*) (Ā. Sū. 7-7).

Sūkta 22. **NOTE**

Maṇḍala 5, Anuvāka 2, Sūkta 29.

Aṣṭaka 4, Adhyāya 1, Varga 23, 24, 25.

Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15.

Seer: गौरिवीति शाक्त्यः (*gaurivīti śāktyaḥ*)
